<script nonce="">
(function () {
    if (window.__ssCartAlertBooted) return;
    window.__ssCartAlertBooted = true;

    var itemsObserver = null;
    var debounceTimer = null;
    var isUpdating = false;

    function onCartPage() {
        return window.location?.pathname?.includes("/cart");
    }

    function correctLocale() {
        var lang = window.urbn?.vendor?.customer_language;
        return ["en-US", "en-CA", "en-GB"].includes(lang);
    }

    function getCartCount() {
        return document.querySelectorAll('.c-pwa-cart-transactional__item').length;
    }

    function updateAlert() {
        if (!onCartPage()) return;
        var heading = document.querySelector("div.s-pwa-cart-transactional-heading");
        if (!heading || !heading.parentNode) return;

        // Look for our alert as the immediate previous sibling of the heading.
        var existing = heading.previousElementSibling;
        if (existing && !existing.classList.contains('ss-cart-alert')) existing = null;

        var hasItems = getCartCount() > 0;

        isUpdating = true;
        try {
            if (hasItems && !existing) {
                var cartAlert = document.createElement("div");
                cartAlert.className = "ss-cart-alert";
                cartAlert.innerHTML = `
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="15" viewBox="0 0 16 15" fill="none">
                        <path fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M7.25 12L7.25 6H8.75V12H7.25Z" fill="#367668"/>
                        <circle cx="8" cy="3.75" r="0.75" transform="rotate(-180 8 3.75)" fill="#367668"/>
                        <circle cx="8" cy="7.5" r="7" transform="rotate(-180 8 7.5)" stroke="#367668"/>
                    </svg>
                    <span>The items in your bag won't last long. Don't miss out!</span>
                `;
                // Insert immediately above the heading
                heading.before(cartAlert);
            } else if (!hasItems && existing) {
                existing.remove();
            }
        } finally {
            setTimeout(function () { isUpdating = false; }, 0);
        }
    }

    function debouncedUpdate() {
        if (isUpdating) return;
        clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
        debounceTimer = setTimeout(updateAlert, 200);
    }

    function watchItemsList() {
        if (itemsObserver) { itemsObserver.disconnect(); itemsObserver = null; }
        var itemsList = document.querySelector(".c-pwa-cart-transactional__items");
        if (!itemsList) return;
        itemsObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
            if (isUpdating) return;
            debouncedUpdate();
        });
        itemsObserver.observe(itemsList, { childList: true });
    }

    function init() {
        if (!onCartPage() || !correctLocale()) return;
        updateAlert();
        watchItemsList();
    }

    function teardown() {
        if (itemsObserver) { itemsObserver.disconnect(); itemsObserver = null; }
        clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    }

    // Hydration-done detection: look for Vue's runtime markers on the mount
    // root. These only attach once Vue has hydrated the app.
    function isHydrated() {
        var root = document.querySelector('.c-pwa-cart__transactional-cart');
        if (!root) return false;
        return !!(root.__vnode || root.__vueParentComponent || root.__vue_app__)
            && !!window.urbn?.vendor
            && !!document.querySelector('div.s-pwa-cart-transactional-heading');
    }

    function whenHydrated(callback) {
        if (isHydrated()) { callback(); return; }
        var checks = 0;
        var maxChecks = 100; // ~10s at 100ms
        var iv = setInterval(function () {
            checks++;
            if (isHydrated()) { clearInterval(iv); callback(); }
            else if (checks >= maxChecks) { clearInterval(iv); }
        }, 100);
    }

    // SPA navigations
    document.addEventListener('urbn-spa-ready', function () {
        teardown();
        if (onCartPage()) init();
    });

    // Initial load — whenHydrated handles waiting, no readiness event needed
    if (onCartPage()) whenHydrated(init);
})();
</script>

<script nonce="">
const terrainBannerHTML = `
<div id="pdp-terrain-banner-SS" class="c-pwa-content-module c-pwa-content-module--content-spacer-large-1" style="margin-bottom: 30px">
  <div class="u-pwa-row" data-qa-module-length="1">

function isTerrainPDP() {
  const path = document.location?.pathname || "";
  return /\/shop\/terrain-/.test(path);
}

function fireTerrainBannerRP() {
  try {
    const img = new Image();
    img.src = "/__ssobj/rp.gif?rp=terrain_banner_pageview&t=" + Date.now();
    console.log("[TerrainBanner] RP beacon fired: terrain_banner_pageview");
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[TerrainBanner] RP beacon failed", e);
  }
}

function injectTerrainBanner() {
  console.log("[TerrainBanner] injectTerrainBanner called");

  if (document.getElementById("pdp-terrain-banner-SS")) {
    console.log("[TerrainBanner] Already injected, skipping");
    return true;
  }

  const targets = [
    "#app > div.js-pwa-breakpoints > div.o-pwa-main.o-pwa-view > main > div:nth-child(1)",
    ".o-pwa-main.o-pwa-view > main > div:first-child",
    "main > div:first-child",
  ];

  let target = null;
  let matchedSelector = null;
  for (const sel of targets) {
    const el = document.querySelector(sel);
    if (el) {
      target = el;
      matchedSelector = sel;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!target) {
    console.warn("[TerrainBanner] No target element found from any selector");
    return false;
  }

  console.log("[TerrainBanner] Target found via selector:", matchedSelector);
  target.insertAdjacentHTML("beforebegin", terrainBannerHTML);
  console.log("[TerrainBanner] Banner injected");

  fireTerrainBannerRP();

  return true;
}

let terrainObserver = null;
let terrainObserverTimeout = null;

function watchForTarget() {
  console.log("[TerrainBanner] watchForTarget called, path:", document.location?.pathname);

  if (injectTerrainBanner()) return;

  if (terrainObserver) terrainObserver.disconnect();
  if (terrainObserverTimeout) clearTimeout(terrainObserverTimeout);

  terrainObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
    if (injectTerrainBanner()) {
      terrainObserver.disconnect();
      terrainObserver = null;
      if (terrainObserverTimeout) clearTimeout(terrainObserverTimeout);
    }
  });

  terrainObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  terrainObserverTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
    if (terrainObserver) {
      terrainObserver.disconnect();
      terrainObserver = null;
      console.log("[TerrainBanner] Observer timed out after 15s");
    }
  }, 15000);
}

document.addEventListener('urbn-spa-ready', () => {
  console.log("[TerrainBanner] urbn-spa-ready fired, path:", document.location?.pathname);

  const existing = document.getElementById("pdp-terrain-banner-SS");
  if (existing) {
    console.log("[TerrainBanner] Removing stale injected banner");
    existing.remove();
  }

  if (!isTerrainPDP()) {
    console.log("[TerrainBanner] Not a terrain PDP, skipping");
    return;
  }

  watchForTarget();
});
</script>

function isWeddingPDP() {
  const path = document.location?.pathname || "";
  if (!path.startsWith("/shop/")) return false;
  const keywords = ["bhldn", "wedding", "engage", "bachelorette", "bridal", "bride"];
  const breadcrumbEls = document.querySelectorAll(".o-pwa-breadcrumbs__list-item a, .o-pwa-breadcrumbs__link");
  const breadcrumbTexts = Array.from(breadcrumbEls).map(el => (el.textContent || "").trim().toLowerCase());
  const breadcrumbHrefs = Array.from(breadcrumbEls).map(el => (el.getAttribute("href") || "").toLowerCase());
  const hasMatch = breadcrumbTexts.some(text => keywords.some(kw => text.includes(kw)))
    || breadcrumbHrefs.some(href => keywords.some(kw => href.includes(kw)));
  const hasWeddingOrBhldn = window.urbn?.vendor?.page_breadcrumb?.some(item => {
    const lower = item.toLowerCase();
    return keywords.some(kw => lower.includes(kw));
  });
  const curation = window.urbn?.vendor?.curation_name?.toLowerCase() || "";
  const hasCuration = keywords.some(kw => curation.includes(kw));
  return hasMatch || hasWeddingOrBhldn || hasCuration;
}
function fireAppointmentBannerRP() {
  try {
    const img = new Image();
    img.src = "/__ssobj/rp.gif?rp=appointment_banner_pageview&t=" + Date.now();
  } catch (e) {}
}
function injectAppointmentBanner() {
  if (document.getElementById("pdp-weddings-book-an-appointment-banner-SS")) return true;
  const targets = [
    "#app > div.js-pwa-breakpoints > div.o-pwa-main.o-pwa-view > main > div:nth-child(1)",
    ".o-pwa-main.o-pwa-view > main > div:first-child",
    "main > div:first-child",
  ];
  let target = null;
  for (const sel of targets) {
    const el = document.querySelector(sel);
    if (el) {
      target = el;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!target) return false;
  target.insertAdjacentHTML("beforebegin", appointmentBannerHTML);
  fireAppointmentBannerRP();
  return true;
}
let appointmentObserver = null;
let appointmentObserverTimeout = null;
function watchForAppointmentTarget() {
  if (injectAppointmentBanner()) return;
  if (appointmentObserver) appointmentObserver.disconnect();
  if (appointmentObserverTimeout) clearTimeout(appointmentObserverTimeout);
  appointmentObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
    if (injectAppointmentBanner()) {
      appointmentObserver.disconnect();
      appointmentObserver = null;
      if (appointmentObserverTimeout) clearTimeout(appointmentObserverTimeout);
    }
  });
  appointmentObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  appointmentObserverTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
    if (appointmentObserver) {
      appointmentObserver.disconnect();
      appointmentObserver = null;
    }
  }, 15000);
}
document.addEventListener('urbn-spa-ready', () => {
  const existing = document.getElementById("pdp-weddings-book-an-appointment-banner-SS");
  if (existing) existing.remove();
  if (window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval) {
    clearInterval(window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval);
    window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval = null;
  }
  let attempts = 0;
  const maxAttempts = 20;
  window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval = setInterval(() => {
    attempts++;
    if (isWeddingPDP()) {
      clearInterval(window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval);
      window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval = null;
      watchForAppointmentTarget();
      return;
    }
    if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
      clearInterval(window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval);
      window.__appointmentEligibilityInterval = null;
    }
  }, 300);
});

<script>
(function () {
    if (window.ssDressesNavMetricBound) return;
    window.ssDressesNavMetricBound = true;

    var DRESS_SLUGS = ['dresses-1', 'dress-shop-external'];
    var HOVER_INTENT_MS = 100;
    var DESKTOP_MQ = '(min-width: 1025px)';
    var hoverTimer = null;
    var lastHoveredSlug = null;

    function fireMetric() {
        if (typeof SS !== 'undefined' && SS.EventTrack && SS.EventTrack.metric) {
            SS.EventTrack.metric('dresses_nav_open');
        }
    }

    function getDressLi(target) {
        if (!target || !target.closest) return null;
        var li = target.closest('li.c-pwa-header-navigation__item');
        if (!li) return null;
        var slug = li.getAttribute('data-nav-slug');
        return DRESS_SLUGS.indexOf(slug) !== -1 ? li : null;
    }

    function attachListeners() {
        var nav = document.querySelector('nav.c-pwa-header-navigation-outer');
        if (!nav || nav.dataset.ssDressesBound) return false;
        nav.dataset.ssDressesBound = '1';

        // Desktop: hover with 100ms intent delay (matches SuperNav's own logic)
        nav.addEventListener('mouseover', function (e) {
            if (!window.matchMedia(DESKTOP_MQ).matches) return;
            var li = getDressLi(e.target);
            if (!li) return;
            var slug = li.getAttribute('data-nav-slug');
            if (slug === lastHoveredSlug) return; // already in this li
            lastHoveredSlug = slug;
            clearTimeout(hoverTimer);
            hoverTimer = setTimeout(fireMetric, HOVER_INTENT_MS);
        });

        nav.addEventListener('mouseout', function (e) {
            if (!window.matchMedia(DESKTOP_MQ).matches) return;
            var li = getDressLi(e.target);
            if (!li) return;
            // Only reset if mouse actually left the li (not moved within it)
            if (e.relatedTarget && li.contains(e.relatedTarget)) return;
            clearTimeout(hoverTimer);
            lastHoveredSlug = null;
        });

        // Mobile: click/tap fires the metric immediately
        nav.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            if (window.matchMedia(DESKTOP_MQ).matches) return;
            var li = getDressLi(e.target);
            if (!li) return;
            fireMetric();
        });

        return true;
    }

    // Try immediately, then wait for SPA ready, then poll briefly as a backstop
    if (!attachListeners()) {
        document.addEventListener('urbn-spa-ready', attachListeners);
        var attempts = 0;
        var poll = setInterval(function () {
            if (attachListeners() || ++attempts > 20) clearInterval(poll);
        }, 250);
    }
})();
</script>

  function getState() {
    var app = window.urbn && window.urbn.app;
    var st = app && app.$store && app.$store.$pinia && app.$store.$pinia.state && app.$store.$pinia.state.value;
    return (st && st.catalog && st.catalog.products) ? st : null;
  }

  function getEntry(state) {
    var slug = (location.pathname.match(/\/shop\/([^\/\?]+)/) || [])[1];
    var entry = state.catalog.products[slug];
    return (entry && entry.productId) ? { slug: slug, entry: entry } : null;
  }

  // FALLBACK: average recent reviews' fit slider already present in Pinia
  function pinaLabel(entry) {
    var lr = entry.reviews && entry.reviews.latestReviews;
    if (!lr || !lr.length) return null;
    var vals = [];
    lr.forEach(function (r) {
      var f = r && r.secondaryRatings && r.secondaryRatings.fitslide;
      if (f && typeof f.value === 'number') vals.push(f.value);
    });
    if (!vals.length) return null;
    var avg = vals.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / vals.length;
    return labelFromAverage(avg);
  }

  // PRIMARY: accurate all-reviews aggregate via the app's a15 client
  function a15Label(state, entry) {
    var app = window.urbn && window.urbn.app;
    var svc = app && app.$a15Svc;
    if (!svc || !svc.a15Client || !svc.a15Client.get) return Promise.reject(new Error('no a15 client'));

    var mc = '';
    (function find(o, d) {
      if (mc || d > 6 || !o || typeof o !== 'object') return;
      for (var k in o) { if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(o, k)) continue;
        if (k === 'merchandiseClass' && typeof o[k] === 'string') { mc = o[k]; return; }
        if (o[k] && typeof o[k] === 'object') find(o[k], d + 1);
      }
    })(entry, 0);

    var sid = 'uo-us', cur = 'USD', lang = 'en-US';
    for (var sk in state) {
      var s = state[sk];
      if (s && s.siteGroupDefaults) {
        var g = s.siteGroupDefaults;
        if (g.defaultSiteId) sid = g.defaultSiteId;
        if (g.currencies && g.currencies[sid]) cur = g.currencies[sid];
        if (g.languages && g.languages[sid]) lang = g.languages[sid];
      }
    }
    var opts = {
      urbn: { cache: true },
      headers: { 'X-Urbn-Site-Id': sid, 'X-Urbn-Currency': cur, 'X-Urbn-Language': lang },
      params: { 'merchandise-class': mc, offset: 0, limit: 1, sort: 'submissionTime:desc' }
    };
    return svc.a15Client
      .get('/review/v0/' + sid + '/products/' + entry.productId + '/reviews', opts)
      .then(function (res) {
        var d = (res && res.data) ? res.data : res;
        var fs = d && d.product && d.product.reviewStatistics
          && d.product.reviewStatistics.secondaryRatingsAverages
          && d.product.reviewStatistics.secondaryRatingsAverages.fitSlide;
        return labelFromAverage(fs && fs.averageRating);
      });
  }

  function render(slug) {
    var label = cache[slug];
    if (!label || label === 'PENDING') return;
    var current = (location.pathname.match(/\/shop\/([^\/\?]+)/) || [])[1];
    if (current !== slug) return;

    var existing = document.getElementById(CONFIG.ID);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.dataset.productSlug === slug) return;
      existing.parentNode.removeChild(existing);
    }
    var anchor = document.querySelector(ANCHOR_BEFORE);
    var placement = 'before';
    if (!anchor) { anchor = document.querySelector(ANCHOR_AFTER); placement = 'after'; }
    if (!anchor || !anchor.parentNode) { dbg('anchor not found yet'); return; }

    var line = document.createElement('div');
    line.id = CONFIG.ID;
    line.dataset.productSlug = slug;
    line.style.cssText = CONFIG.STYLE;
    
    // Commented out to ensure Avenir CSS styling sticks
    // var fontRef = document.querySelector('.c-pwa-legend, legend, .c-pwa-radio-boxes__label');
    // if (fontRef) line.style.fontFamily = getComputedStyle(fontRef).fontFamily;
    
    line.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Customers Say: '));
    var strong = document.createElement('strong');
    strong.textContent = label;
    strong.style.setProperty('font-weight', '350', 'important');
    line.appendChild(strong);

    if (placement === 'before') anchor.parentNode.insertBefore(line, anchor);
    else anchor.parentNode.insertBefore(line, anchor.nextSibling);
    dbg('INJECTED', placement, '=> Customers Say:', label);
  }

  function tick() {
    if (!/\/shop\//.test(location.pathname)) return;
    var state = getState();
    if (!state) { dbg('pinia/catalog not ready'); return; }
    var found = getEntry(state);
    if (!found) { dbg('product not ready'); return; }
    var slug = found.slug, entry = found.entry;

    if (cache[slug] === undefined) {
      cache[slug] = 'PENDING';
      a15Label(state, entry).then(function (label) {
        cache[slug] = label || pinaLabel(entry);
        dbg('resolved via', label ? 'a15 (accurate)' : 'pinia fallback', '->', cache[slug]);
        render(slug);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        cache[slug] = pinaLabel(entry);
        dbg('a15 failed (' + (e && e.message) + '); pinia fallback ->', cache[slug]);
        render(slug);
      });
    }
    render(slug);
  }

  // Safely initialize the script once the DOM is ready to prevent timing errors
  function init() {
    setInterval(tick, 300);
    tick();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  } else {
    init();
  }
})();
}
</script>

<script nonce="" type="text/javascript" data-sitespect="true">
window.__sscf.cf_11052315  = function() {
(function () {
  'use strict';

  // ---- Shared config ----
  var TILE_SEL   = '.c-pwa-tile-grid-tile';
  var LINK_SEL   = 'a.o-pwa-product-tile__link[href*="/shop/"]';
  var ATTR_STYLE = 'data-style-number';
  var ATTR_SLUG  = 'data-product-slug';
  var CART_ITEM_SEL = '.c-pwa-cart-transactional__item';

  // ---- MODULE 1: Tile stamping (category + search) ----
  function tileRecords() {
    var app = window.urbn && window.urbn.app;
    var pinia = app && ((app.$store && app.$store.$pinia) || app.$pinia);
    var state = pinia && pinia.state && pinia.state.value;
    if (!state) return [];
    var out = [];
    ['category', 'search'].forEach(function (key) {
      var pages = state[key] && state[key].pages;
      if (!pages) return;
      Object.keys(pages).forEach(function (p) {
        var t = pages[p] && pages[p].wrapper && pages[p].wrapper.tiles;
        if (t && t.length) { for (var i = 0; i < t.length; i++) out.push(t[i]); }
      });
    });
    return out;
  }

  function slugFromHref(href) {
    if (!href) return '';
    var m = /\/shop\/([^/?#]+)/.exec(href);
    return m ? m[1].toLowerCase() : '';
  }

  function buildMap(recs) {
    var map = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < recs.length; i++) {
      var p = (recs[i] && recs[i].product) || {};
      var slug = p.productSlug ? String(p.productSlug).toLowerCase() : '';
      var style = p.styleNumber ? String(p.styleNumber) : '';
      if (slug && style && !map[slug]) map[slug] = style;
    }
    return map;
  }

  function stamp() {
    var recs = tileRecords();
    if (!recs.length) return false;
    var map = buildMap(recs);
    var tiles = document.querySelectorAll(TILE_SEL);
    for (var i = 0; i < tiles.length; i++) {
      var tile = tiles[i];
      if (tile.getAttribute(ATTR_STYLE)) continue;
      var link = tile.querySelector(LINK_SEL);
      if (!link) continue;
      var slug = slugFromHref(link.getAttribute('href'));
      var style = slug && map[slug];
      if (!style) continue;
      tile.setAttribute(ATTR_STYLE, style);
      tile.setAttribute(ATTR_SLUG, slug);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // ---- MODULE 2: Monetate cart retrack ----
  if (typeof window.trackMonetateCart === 'undefined') {
    var debounce = function (func, wait) {
      var timeout;
      return function () {
        var args = arguments, ctx = this;
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        timeout = setTimeout(function () { func.apply(ctx, args); }, wait);
      };
    };

    window.trackMonetateCart = debounce(function () {
      try {
        var addRowsArray = [];
        var addProductsArray = [];
        document.querySelectorAll(CART_ITEM_SEL).forEach(function (element) {
          var styleNumEl = element.querySelector('[data-style-number]');
          var priceEl = element.querySelector('.c-pwa-item-price__unit');
          var qtyEl = element.querySelector('.c-pwa-quantity-selector select');
          if (!styleNumEl || !priceEl || !qtyEl) return;
          var styleNum = styleNumEl.getAttribute('data-style-number');
          var unitPrice = priceEl.innerText.replace(/[^\d.]/g, '');
          var quantity = qtyEl.value;
          if (styleNum && unitPrice && quantity) {
            addRowsArray.push({
              productId: styleNum,
              quantity: quantity,
              unitPrice: unitPrice,
              currency: "USD"
            });
            addProductsArray.push(styleNum);
          }
        });
        console.log("addRowsArray", addRowsArray);
        console.log("addProductsArray", addProductsArray);
        if (addRowsArray.length > 0) {
          window.monetateQ = window.monetateQ || [];
          window.monetateQ.push(["setPageType", "cart"]);
          window.monetateQ.push(["addCartRows", addRowsArray]);
          window.monetateQ.push(["addProducts", addProductsArray]);
          window.monetateQ.push(["trackData"]);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Monetate Cart Tracking Error:", e);
      }
    }, 500);
  }

  // ---- Runner: fires both modules where relevant ----
  function runAll() {
    stamp();
    if (document.querySelector(CART_ITEM_SEL)) {
      window.trackMonetateCart();
    }
  }

  // ---- Initial settle ----
  var tries = 0;
  (function settle() {
    var stamped = stamp();
    if (document.querySelector(CART_ITEM_SEL)) window.trackMonetateCart();
    if (stamped || ++tries > 40) { watch(); return; }
    setTimeout(settle, 150);
  })();

  // ---- SPA navigation + DOM watcher ----
  function watch() {
    var pending = null;
    function queue() {
      if (pending) return;
      pending = setTimeout(function () { pending = null; runAll(); }, 120);
    }
    if (window.MutationObserver && document.body) {
      new MutationObserver(queue).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    ['pushState', 'replaceState'].forEach(function (m) {
      var orig = history[m];
      if (typeof orig !== 'function') return;
      history[m] = function () {
        var r = orig.apply(this, arguments);
        setTimeout(runAll, 400);
        return r;
      };
    });
    window.addEventListener('popstate', function () { setTimeout(runAll, 400); });
  }

  window.__urbnStampTiles = stamp;
})();
}
</script>

<script nonce="" type="text/javascript" data-sitespect="true">
window.__sscf.cf_11016335  = function() {
(function() {
  if (window.ssFitQsInit) return;
  window.ssFitQsInit = true;

  var QSCFG = {
    ID: 'ss-fit-summary-qs',
    DEBUG: false, // set false before shipping
    STYLE: "display:block;width:100%;margin:12px 0;font-family:'Avenir', sans-serif;font-style:normal;font-weight:350;font-size:13px;line-height:18px;letter-spacing:0.6px;color:#5C5C5F;"
  };

  var QS_FIT = {
    1: 'Runs small',
    2: 'Runs a little small',
    3: 'True to size',
    4: 'Runs a little large',
    5: 'Runs large'
  };

  var qsCache = {}; // slug -> label | null | 'PENDING'

  function qsLog() { if (QSCFG.DEBUG) { try { console.log.apply(console, ['[ss-fit-qs]'].concat([].slice.call(arguments))); } catch (e) {} } }

  // The edit-from-cart quickshop's primary action is "Update Item" (vs "Add to Cart").
  function qsIsCartEditModal(modal) {
    var btns = modal.querySelectorAll('button');
    for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) {
      if (/update\s*(item|bag)/i.test(btns[i].textContent || '')) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function qsLabelFromAvg(avg) {
    if (typeof avg !== 'number') return null;
    var n = Math.min(5, Math.max(Math.ceil(((avg - 1) * 5) / 4), 1));
    return QS_FIT[n] || null;
  }

  function qsState() {
    var app = window.urbn && window.urbn.app;
    var st = app && app.$store && app.$store.$pinia && app.$store.$pinia.state && app.$store.$pinia.state.value;
    return (st && st.catalog && st.catalog.products) ? st : null;
  }

  function qsModalSlug(modal) {
    var link = modal.querySelector('.c-pwa-family-product__heading-link, .c-pwa-family-product__details-link');
    var href = link ? link.getAttribute('href') : '';
    var m = href && href.match(/\/shop\/([^\/\?]+)/);
    return m && m[1];
  }

  function qsPinaLabel(entry) {
    var lr = entry.reviews && entry.reviews.latestReviews;
    if (!lr || !lr.length) return null;
    var vals = [];
    lr.forEach(function (r) {
      var f = r && r.secondaryRatings && r.secondaryRatings.fitslide;
      if (f && typeof f.value === 'number') vals.push(f.value);
    });
    if (!vals.length) return null;
    var avg = vals.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / vals.length;
    return qsLabelFromAvg(avg);
  }

  function qsA15Label(state, entry) {
    var app = window.urbn && window.urbn.app;
    var svc = app && app.$a15Svc;
    if (!svc || !svc.a15Client || !svc.a15Client.get) return Promise.reject(new Error('no a15 client'));

    var mc = '';
    (function find(o, d) {
      if (mc || d > 6 || !o || typeof o !== 'object') return;
      for (var k in o) { if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(o, k)) continue;
        if (k === 'merchandiseClass' && typeof o[k] === 'string') { mc = o[k]; return; }
        if (o[k] && typeof o[k] === 'object') find(o[k], d + 1);
      }
    })(entry, 0);

    var sid = 'uo-us', cur = 'USD', lang = 'en-US';
    for (var sk in state) {
      var s = state[sk];
      if (s && s.siteGroupDefaults) {
        var g = s.siteGroupDefaults;
        if (g.defaultSiteId) sid = g.defaultSiteId;
        if (g.currencies && g.currencies[sid]) cur = g.currencies[sid];
        if (g.languages && g.languages[sid]) lang = g.languages[sid];
      }
    }
    var opts = {
      urbn: { cache: true },
      headers: { 'X-Urbn-Site-Id': sid, 'X-Urbn-Currency': cur, 'X-Urbn-Language': lang },
      params: { 'merchandise-class': mc, offset: 0, limit: 1, sort: 'submissionTime:desc' }
    };
    return svc.a15Client
      .get('/review/v0/' + sid + '/products/' + entry.productId + '/reviews', opts)
      .then(function (res) {
        var d = (res && res.data) ? res.data : res;
        var fs = d && d.product && d.product.reviewStatistics
          && d.product.reviewStatistics.secondaryRatingsAverages
          && d.product.reviewStatistics.secondaryRatingsAverages.fitSlide;
        return qsLabelFromAvg(fs && fs.averageRating);
      });
  }

  function qsRender(slug) {
    var label = qsCache[slug];
    if (!label || label === 'PENDING') return;
    var modal = document.querySelector('.c-pwa-quickshop');
    if (!modal || qsModalSlug(modal) !== slug) return;

    var existing = document.getElementById(QSCFG.ID);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.dataset.productSlug === slug) return;
      existing.parentNode.removeChild(existing);
    }
    var anchor = modal.querySelector('fieldset.c-pwa-sku-selection__size');
    if (!anchor || !anchor.parentNode) { qsLog('size fieldset not found'); return; }

    var line = document.createElement('div');
    line.id = QSCFG.ID;
    line.dataset.productSlug = slug;
    line.style.cssText = QSCFG.STYLE;
    line.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Customers Say: '));
    var strong = document.createElement('strong');
    strong.textContent = label;
    strong.style.setProperty('font-weight', '350', 'important');
    line.appendChild(strong);

    anchor.parentNode.insertBefore(line, anchor.nextSibling);
    qsLog('INJECTED =>', label);
  }

  function qsTick() {
    var modal = document.querySelector('.c-pwa-quickshop');
    if (!modal) return;

    if (qsIsCartEditModal(modal)) { qsLog('skip cart-edit quickshop'); return; }

    var state = qsState();
    if (!state) { qsLog('catalog not ready'); return; }
    var slug = qsModalSlug(modal);
    if (!slug) { qsLog('no modal slug'); return; }
    var entry = state.catalog.products[slug];
    if (!entry || !entry.productId) { qsLog('modal product not in catalog yet', slug); return; }

    if (qsCache[slug] === undefined) {
      qsCache[slug] = 'PENDING';
      qsA15Label(state, entry).then(function (label) {
        qsCache[slug] = label || qsPinaLabel(entry);
        qsLog('resolved via', label ? 'a15 (accurate)' : 'pinia fallback', '->', qsCache[slug]);
        qsRender(slug);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        qsCache[slug] = qsPinaLabel(entry);
        qsLog('a15 failed (' + (e && e.message) + '); pinia fallback ->', qsCache[slug]);
        qsRender(slug);
      });
    }
    qsRender(slug);
  }

  // Safely initialize the script once the DOM is ready to prevent timing errors
  function init() {
    setInterval(qsTick, 300);
    qsTick();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  } else {
    init();
  }
})();
}

window.__sscf.cf_10943414  = function() {
const titles = document.querySelectorAll('.c-pwa-text-component__title, .c-pwa-text-component__pre-title, h2.c-pwa-text-component__title--left');
titles.forEach(title => {
    if (title.textContent.includes('Shop by Fit:')) {

        if (container && !container.classList.contains('is-trending-grid')) {
            container.classList.add('is-trending-grid');

  return window.location.pathname.indexOf(TARGET_PATH) === 0;

  s.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');

var clearContainerStyles = function(container) {

   'scroll-snap-type','flex-wrap','width','max-width','margin','margin-left','margin-right',
   'padding','padding-left','padding-right','box-sizing','transform'].forEach(function(p) {

var applyChildStyles = function(child) {

  s.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
  s.setProperty('float', 'none', 'important');
  s.setProperty('scroll-snap-align', 'none', 'important');
  s.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
  s.setProperty('display', 'block', 'important');
  s.setProperty('grid-column', 'auto', 'important');

  child.querySelectorAll('.o-pwa-editorial__cta-link-outer').forEach(function(ctaOuter) {

    ls.setProperty('text-align', 'center', 'important');

var clearChildStyles = function(child) {
  ['width','max-width','min-width','flex','flex-basis','box-sizing','padding','margin',
   'float','scroll-snap-align','transform','display','grid-column'].forEach(function(p) {
    child.style.removeProperty(p);
  });

    ['text-align','justify-content','align-items'].forEach(function(p) {
      ctaOuter.style.removeProperty(p);
    });
  });

    ['text-align','margin-left','margin-right'].forEach(function(p) {
      ctaLink.style.removeProperty(p);
    });
  });
};

var findTargetSliders = function() {
  var results = [];
  document.querySelectorAll('.c-pwa-content-module--mobile-slider').forEach(function(el) {
    if (el.querySelector('.c-pwa-content-module__component--slider-2up')) results.push(el);
  });
  return results;
};

var enhanceContainer = function(container) {
  applyContainerStyles(container);
  container.querySelectorAll(':scope > .c-pwa-content-module__component').forEach(applyChildStyles);

  if (STATE.containers.indexOf(container) !== -1) return;
  STATE.containers.push(container);

  var mo = new MutationObserver(function() {
    if (!MOBILE_MQ.matches) return;
    applyContainerStyles(container);
    container.querySelectorAll(':scope > .c-pwa-content-module__component').forEach(applyChildStyles);
  });
  mo.observe(container, {
    attributes: true,
    attributeFilter: ['style', 'class'],
    subtree: true,
    childList: true
  });
};

var revertContainer = function(container) {
  clearContainerStyles(container);
  container.querySelectorAll(':scope > .c-pwa-content-module__component').forEach(clearChildStyles);
};

var run = function() {
  if (!isTargetPage()) return;
  if (MOBILE_MQ.matches) {
    findTargetSliders().forEach(enhanceContainer);
  } else {
    STATE.containers.forEach(revertContainer);
  }
};

run();
var attempts = 0;
var poll = setInterval(function() {
  attempts++;
  run();
  if (attempts > 40) clearInterval(poll);
}, 250);

window.addEventListener('urbn-spa-ready', function() {
  setTimeout(run, 150);
  setTimeout(run, 500);
});

var mqHandler = function() { run(); };
if (MOBILE_MQ.addEventListener) {
  MOBILE_MQ.addEventListener('change', mqHandler);
} else if (MOBILE_MQ.addListener) {
  MOBILE_MQ.addListener(mqHandler);
}
}

<script nonce="" type="text/javascript" data-sitespect="true">
window.__sscf.cf_11000855  = function() {
(function() {
    if (window.__ssFitDebug !== false) {
        console.log('[ss-fit] script booted, href=' + location.href);
    }
    // 1. Global Translation Dictionary (Updated Ranges)
    // 5'4 = ~162cm | 5'8 = ~173cm
    const locales = {
        'en': {
            prefix: "Best if your height is ",
            fits: { 'standard': "5'4\" to 5'8\"", 'petite': "below 5'4\"", 'plus': "5'4\" to 5'8\"", 'tall': "above 5'8\"" }
        },
        'en-gb': {
            prefix: "Best if your height is ",
            fits: { 'standard': "5'4\" to 5'8\"", 'petite': "below 5'4\"", 'plus': "5'4\" to 5'8\"", 'tall': "above 5'8\"" }
        },
        'fr': {
            prefix: "Idéal si vous mesurez ",
            fits: { 'standard': "162 cm à 173 cm", 'petite': "moins de 162 cm", 'plus': "162 cm à 173 cm", 'tall': "plus de 173 cm" }
        },
        'de': {
            prefix: "Ideal bei einer Körpergröße von ",
            fits: { 'standard': "162 cm bis 173 cm", 'petite': "unter 162 cm", 'plus': "162 cm bis 173 cm", 'tall': "über 173 cm" }
        },
        'it': {
            prefix: "Ideale se la tua altezza è ",
            fits: { 'standard': "da 162 cm a 173 cm", 'petite': "meno di 162 cm", 'plus': "da 162 cm a 173 cm", 'tall': "più di 173 cm" }
        },
        'es': {
            prefix: "Ideal si mides ",
            fits: { 'standard': "entre 162 cm y 173 cm", 'petite': "menos de 162 cm", 'plus': "entre 162 cm y 173 cm", 'tall': "más de 173 cm" }
        }
    };
    // 2. Universal Locale Sniffer
    function getLocale() {
        const href = window.location.href.toLowerCase();

        if (href.includes('/fr-fr/') || href.includes('.fr/')) return locales['fr'];
        if (href.includes('/de-de/') || href.includes('.de/')) return locales['de'];
        if (href.includes('/it-it/') || href.includes('.it/')) return locales['it'];
        if (href.includes('/es-es/') || href.includes('.es/')) return locales['es'];
        if (href.includes('/en-gb/') || href.includes('.uk/')) return locales['en-gb'];

        try {
            let lang = document.documentElement.lang.toLowerCase();
            if (lang.includes('fr')) return locales['fr'];
            if (lang.includes('de')) return locales['de'];
            if (lang.includes('it')) return locales['it'];
            if (lang.includes('es')) return locales['es'];
            if (lang === 'en-gb') return locales['en-gb'];
        } catch(e) {}

        return locales['en'];
    }
    // 2a. Fast path filter — only PDPs have the fit fieldset. Everything else
    // (/clothes, /shop with no product slug, home, cart, etc.) should short-
    // circuit before we ever touch the DOM or log anything.
    function isPDPPath() {
        // Anthro PDPs live under /shop/<slug>. A bare /shop or /shop/ is a
        // browse page, not a product.
        return /\/shop\/[^\/?#]+/i.test(location.pathname);
    }
    // 2b. Bottoms PDP detector — reads breadcrumb link hrefs
    const BOTTOMS_HREF_REGEX = /\/bottoms/i;
    const BOTTOMS_TEXT_REGEX = /(jean|pant|trouser|jogger|legging|short|skirt|denim|chino|cargo|jupe|hose|pantalon|falda)/i;
    function isBottomsPDP() {
        const breadcrumbs = document.querySelector('.o-pwa-breadcrumbs');
        if (!breadcrumbs) return false;

        const links = breadcrumbs.querySelectorAll('.o-pwa-breadcrumbs__link');
        for (let i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
            const href = links[i].getAttribute('href') || '';
            if (BOTTOMS_HREF_REGEX.test(href)) return true;
        }

        const text = breadcrumbs.textContent || '';
        return BOTTOMS_TEXT_REGEX.test(text);
    }
    // DEBUG: hardcoded on for now so it survives full page reloads —
    // flip to false once resolved. Filter the console by "ss-fit" to
    // cut through unrelated site logging.
    window.__ssFitDebug = true;
    // Dedupe log calls — the mutation observer + idle poll would otherwise
    // fire the same "no fitFieldset" message dozens of times per second.
    // Only emit when the (tag, extra) pair changes.
    let __ssFitLastLogKey = '';
    window.__ssFitDebugLog = function(tag, extra) {
        if (!window.__ssFitDebug) return;
        let key;
        try {
            key = tag + '|' + (extra !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(extra) : '') + '|' + location.href;
        } catch (e) {
            key = tag + '|' + String(extra) + '|' + location.href;
        }
        if (key === __ssFitLastLogKey) return;
        __ssFitLastLogKey = key;
        console.log('[ss-fit]', tag, extra !== undefined ? extra : '', 'href=' + location.href);
    };
    // 3. Main Render Function
    function renderFitText() {
        // Hard bail if we're not on a PDP URL at all — no point querying the
        // DOM or logging on /clothes, /shop browse pages, home, etc.
        if (!isPDPPath()) return;

        const fitFieldset = document.querySelector('.c-pwa-sku-selection__fits-outer');
        if (!fitFieldset) {
            window.__ssFitDebugLog('no fitFieldset (.c-pwa-sku-selection__fits-outer not found)');
            return;
        }
        if (!isBottomsPDP()) {
            window.__ssFitDebugLog('isBottomsPDP() false — breadcrumbs not matching bottoms');
            const existingSpan = document.getElementById('ss-fit-height-text');
            if (existingSpan) existingSpan.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        const labelContainer = fitFieldset.querySelector('.c-pwa-legend, legend');
        const inputs = fitFieldset.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]');

        if (!labelContainer || inputs.length === 0) {
            window.__ssFitDebugLog('missing labelContainer or inputs', { labelContainer: !!labelContainer, inputCount: inputs.length });
            return;
        }
        let selectedInput = null;
        const localeData = getLocale();

        for (let i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) {
            if (inputs[i].checked || inputs[i].getAttribute('aria-checked') === 'true') {
                selectedInput = inputs[i];
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!selectedInput && inputs[0]) {
            selectedInput = inputs[0];
        }

        if (!selectedInput) {
            window.__ssFitDebugLog('no selectedInput found at all');
            return;
        }
        const inputId = selectedInput.id ? selectedInput.id.toLowerCase() : '';
        const inputValue = selectedInput.value ? selectedInput.value.toLowerCase() : '';
        let labelText = '';

        if (selectedInput.id) {
            const correspondingLabel = fitFieldset.querySelector(`label[for="${selectedInput.id}"]`);
            if (correspondingLabel) labelText = correspondingLabel.textContent.toLowerCase();
        }
        const combinedString = `${inputId} ${inputValue} ${labelText}`;
        let mappedFit = '';

        if (/(petite|klein|corto|courte)/.test(combinedString)) mappedFit = 'petite';
        else if (/(plus|curve|forti|grandes)/.test(combinedString)) mappedFit = 'plus';
        else if (/(tall|long|lang|alto|groß|gross)/.test(combinedString)) mappedFit = 'tall';
        else if (/(standard|regular|normal)/.test(combinedString)) mappedFit = 'standard';
        const textToShow = (mappedFit && localeData.fits[mappedFit]) ? (localeData.prefix + localeData.fits[mappedFit]) : '';
        window.__ssFitDebugLog('computed match', { combinedString, mappedFit, textToShow });
        let customSpan = document.getElementById('ss-fit-height-text');

        if (!textToShow) {
            if (customSpan) customSpan.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        let isNewSpan = false;
        // Guard against Vue having replaced/removed the labelContainer node
        // (getElementById can still return a detached span in edge cases)
        if (customSpan && !document.body.contains(customSpan)) {
            customSpan = null;
        }
        if (!customSpan) {
            customSpan = document.createElement('span');
            customSpan.id = 'ss-fit-height-text';
            labelContainer.appendChild(customSpan);

            labelContainer.style.display = 'inline-block';
            labelContainer.style.width = 'max-content';
            labelContainer.style.position = 'relative';
            labelContainer.style.overflow = 'visible';

            isNewSpan = true;
        } else if (customSpan.parentNode !== labelContainer) {
            // labelContainer was re-rendered by Vue and is a new node —
            // re-attach our span to the current, live container.
            labelContainer.appendChild(customSpan);
        }
        if (isNewSpan || customSpan.innerText !== textToShow) {
            customSpan.innerText = textToShow;
            customSpan.style.display = 'inline-block';
            customSpan.style.position = 'absolute';
            customSpan.style.left = '100%';
            customSpan.style.top = '0';
            customSpan.style.marginLeft = '5px';
            customSpan.style.whiteSpace = 'nowrap';

            customSpan.style.fontFamily = "'Avenir', sans-serif";
            customSpan.style.fontStyle = "italic";
            customSpan.style.fontWeight = "350";
            customSpan.style.color = "#5C5C5F";
            customSpan.style.letterSpacing = "0.6px";
            customSpan.style.fontSize = window.innerWidth >= 768 ? "13px" : "11px";
            customSpan.style.lineHeight = window.innerWidth >= 768 ? "18px" : "16px";
        }
    }
    // 4. Burst-polling helper — runs every 100ms for ~4s, then hands off to
    // a slower background poll. Can be re-armed (see below) any time we know
    // navigation just happened, so we don't depend on any single mutation or
    // app-dispatched event landing at exactly the right moment.
    // Skips entirely on non-PDP paths so /clothes, /shop browse, home, etc.
    // don't run an interval forever.
    let burstInterval = null;
    let idleInterval = null;
    function armBurstPolling() {
        if (idleInterval) { clearInterval(idleInterval); idleInterval = null; }
        if (burstInterval) { clearInterval(burstInterval); burstInterval = null; }
        if (!isPDPPath()) return;
        let ticks = 0;
        burstInterval = setInterval(function() {
            renderFitText();
            ticks++;
            if (ticks > 40) { // ~4s
                clearInterval(burstInterval);
                burstInterval = null;
                // Only keep an idle poll alive if we're still on a PDP URL.
                if (isPDPPath()) {
                    idleInterval = setInterval(function() {
                        if (!isPDPPath()) {
                            clearInterval(idleInterval);
                            idleInterval = null;
                            return;
                        }
                        renderFitText();
                    }, 250);
                }
            }
        }, 100);
    }
    // 5. Reactive triggers
    // a) React to any DOM mutation (Vue reconciling the fieldset/breadcrumbs,
    //    default fit selection being applied, etc.). Cheap early-out on
    //    non-PDP paths so browse pages don't churn.
    const observer = new MutationObserver(function() {
        if (!isPDPPath()) return;
        renderFitText();
    });
    observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true,
        attributes: true,
        attributeFilter: ['checked', 'aria-checked', 'class']
    });
    // b) React to the app's own SPA-navigation-complete signal, if it fires
    document.addEventListener('urbn-spa-ready', armBurstPolling);
    // c) React to the user actually changing the fit selection
    document.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
        if (e.target && e.target.matches && e.target.matches('.c-pwa-sku-selection__fits-outer input[type="radio"]')) {
            renderFitText();
        }
    }, true);
    // d) Cover the plain full-load case too
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', armBurstPolling);
    window.addEventListener('load', armBurstPolling);
    // e) Detect SPA route changes directly, independent of any app event.
    //    Vue Router navigations go through pushState/replaceState (and
    //    popstate for back/forward) — wrapping these catches every
    //    client-side navigation regardless of whether urbn-spa-ready fires.
    let lastHref = location.href;
    function onLocationChange() {
        if (location.href !== lastHref) {
            lastHref = location.href;
            // Reset the log-dedup key so the first message on a new URL
            // always prints, even if it's the same tag as the previous page.
            __ssFitLastLogKey = '';
            armBurstPolling();
        }
    }
    (function(history) {
        const push = history.pushState;
        const replace = history.replaceState;
        history.pushState = function() {
            const ret = push.apply(history, arguments);
            onLocationChange();
            return ret;
        };
        history.replaceState = function() {
            const ret = replace.apply(history, arguments);
            onLocationChange();
            return ret;
        };
    })(window.history);
    window.addEventListener('popstate', onLocationChange);
    // Belt-and-suspenders: cheap periodic check for URL drift the above
    // might miss (e.g. router implementations that mutate location in
    // less common ways).
    setInterval(onLocationChange, 500);
    // Run once immediately in case the DOM is already there on hard load.
    // armBurstPolling() will no-op if we're not on a /shop/ URL.
    renderFitText();
    armBurstPolling();
})();
}

</span><!--]--></button></h3><div class="o-pwa-accordion__panel"><!--[--><!--[--><div class="c-pwa-text-component o-pwa-contentful-accordion__component" isnestedgrid="true" aligntitletogrid="false"><div class="s-pwa-cms c-pwa-markdown c-pwa-text-component__body-copy--left c-pwa-text-component__body-copy c-pwa-text-component__body-copy--default" style=""><ul>
<li>Returns or exchanges must be accompanied by an original sales receipt or AnthroPerks lookup.</li>
<li>We'll gladly accept unworn, unwashed, and unaltered merchandise for return or exchange within one year of purchase or delivery date of shipped items. All tags must be attached, and items must be returned in original packaging. Defective items may still be accepted.</li>
<li>For merchandise returned <em>within</em> 30 days of the delivery or in-store purchase date, a refund will be issued to the original form of payment at the original selling price. If returned <em>after</em> 30 days, a merchandise credit will be issued at the original selling price. After one year, we will no longer accept returns or exchanges. For online orders, the window for returns and exchanges will be determined by the delivery date of the shipped items.</li>
<li>A one-time price adjustment will be granted on full-price purchases only if an original sales receipt is presented within 14 days of purchase.</li>
<li>All items can be returned to stores with the exception of Curated by Anthropologie merchandise, Unlimited Furniture Delivery items, and wedding gowns and bridal sets <em>unless they were originally purchased at a store location</em>. Please see below for more information on these exception policies. </li>
<li>Items purchased online or in-store cannot be returned to the Anthropologie Home Outlet or Anthropologie pop-up locations.</li>
</ul>
<br><br><h3>Final Sale</h3>
<ul>
<li>All sale items ending in $.95 that are purchased at an additional sale-on-sale discount and marked "Final Sale," cannot be returned or exchanged.</li>
<li>Purchases of packaged food, menstrual cups, personal devices from our sexual wellness assortment, live plants, and made-to-order wedding gowns and bridal sets cannot be returned or exchanged.</li>
<li>All items sold at Anthropologie Home Outlets are sold as-is and are final sale.</li>
</ul>

</span><!--]--></button></h3><div class="o-pwa-accordion__panel"><!--[--><!--[--><div class="c-pwa-text-component o-pwa-contentful-accordion__component" isnestedgrid="true" aligntitletogrid="false"><div class="s-pwa-cms c-pwa-markdown c-pwa-text-component__body-copy--left c-pwa-text-component__body-copy c-pwa-text-component__body-copy--default" style=""><h3>Returns</h3>
To return or exchange merchandise purchased on anthropologie.com, our iOS app, or by phone,  follow the easy steps below!
<br><br>Please note, merchandise purchased in our stores can only be returned to a store. Return labels are only available for orders shipping to the continental US and no additional postage is required. However, a $5.95 shipping charge will be deducted from your refund unless you are processing an exchange. Shipping fees are non-refundable.
<br><br>Anthropologie is not responsible for non-Anthropologie items shipped back to us in error.
<br><br><h4>1. Locate Your Order</h4>
If you have an Anthropologie account, sign in, select <a href="/orders">Order History</a>, then locate and select the order you wish to return or exchange. Guest orders and orders received as gifts may be found by checking your <a href="#order-inquiry">Order Status</a>.
<br><a href="#order-inquiry">Begin Online Return or Exchange</a>
<br><br><h4>2. Start a Return or Exchange &amp; Print Your Return Label</h4>
Once you've located your order, select Start a Return or Exchange and follow the instructions to print your label. Upon completion, you will receive an email with tracking information, as well as a copy of your label in case it is misplaced.
<br><br><h4>3. Affix Return Label</h4>
Affix the return label to your package, and be sure to remove (or cover) the original shipping label. For a faster refund, your return label and return slip should correspond with the items in your package. Please process and mail multiple returns individually.
<br><a href="https://wwwapps.ups.com/pickup/schedule?loc=en_US" target="_blank">Schedule a Complimentary UPS Pickup</a>
<br><br>If you prefer to use your own carrier, please use a trackable and insured shipping method. Anthropologie is not responsible for shipping fees or lost returns. Don't forget to obtain a tracking number and a receipt from the carrier for your records.
<br><br><h4>Return for a damaged, missing, or wrong item(s)</h4>
We apologize for any issues with your order. Please submit a claim here within 30 days of the shipping date to start your return. 
<br><br><h3>Exchanges</h3>
We offer the exchange options listed below.
<br><br><h4>Exchange for the Same Item in a Different Size or Color</h4>
Did you order the wrong size or color? We’ll provide a FREE return label and waive shipping for your new order. Select "Begin Online Return or Exchange" below.
<br><br><h4>Exchange for a Different Item</h4>
If you’d like to exchange your order for a different item(s), please visit your <a href="/orders">Order History</a> and select “Start a Return or Exchange”.<br>Once you have finished the return process for the selected item(s), feel free to continue shopping and place a new order for any newly selected styles!
<br><br><h4>Exchange for a damaged, missing, or wrong item(s)</h4>
We apologize for any issues with your order. Please <a href="#order-inquiry">submit a claim here</a> within 30 days of the shipping date to initiate a free replacement.
<br><br><h4><a href="/orders">Begin Online Return or Exchange</a></h4>
<br>

<h3>Refund Process</h3>
Most returns will process in approximately 1-2 weeks, depending on your method of return. Additional delays may occur. You will receive an email notification when your return has been processed (be sure to check your spam folder).
<br><br>A refund will then be credited back to your original method of payment and will post approximately 1-3 business days after the processing date. It may take up to two billing cycles for the credit to appear on your monthly credit card statement. Gift Card purchases will be refunded in the form of a merchandise credit.</div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div></div><!--]--></div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div><div id="returns-and-exchanges-9-in-store-purchases" class="c-pwa-content-module c-pwa-content-module--content-spacer-none"><div class="u-pwa-row--nested u-pwa-row" data-qa-module-length="1"><!----><!--[--><!--[--><div class="u-pwa-extra-small--12 c-pwa-content-module__component--stacked c-pwa-content-module__component"><div style="min-height:auto;" class="c-pwa-content-module__lazy-load"><!--[--><div id="1Vd3zQoLfzvxOyQzIvU42E" class="s-pwa-contentful-accordion o-pwa-contentful-accordion" isnestedgrid="true" inline="true" aligntitletogrid="false"><div class="o-pwa-accordion"><!----><h3 class="o-pwa-accordion__heading-outer"><button class="o-pwa-button-none o-pwa-accordion__toggle" type="button" aria-expanded="false"><!--[--><div class="o-pwa-accordion__headings"><span class="o-pwa-accordion__heading"><!----> Returning In-Store Purchases</span><span class="o-pwa-accordion__subheading"></span></div><span class="c-pwa-icon o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded"><svg version="1.1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 15 15" style="enable-background:new 0 0 15 15;" xml:space="preserve">

<br><br><h3>Exchanges</h3>
Exchanges can be made in our stores  within one year of purchase or delivery date of shipped items. </div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div></div><!--]--></div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div><div id="returns-and-exchanges-8-furniture" class="c-pwa-content-module c-pwa-content-module--content-spacer-none"><div class="u-pwa-row--nested u-pwa-row" data-qa-module-length="1"><!----><!--[--><!--[--><div class="u-pwa-extra-small--12 c-pwa-content-module__component--stacked c-pwa-content-module__component"><div style="min-height:auto;" class="c-pwa-content-module__lazy-load"><!--[--><div id="3QqdgnAzC6wmffMuhH45n5" class="s-pwa-contentful-accordion o-pwa-contentful-accordion" isnestedgrid="true" inline="true" aligntitletogrid="false"><div class="o-pwa-accordion"><!----><h3 class="o-pwa-accordion__heading-outer"><button class="o-pwa-button-none o-pwa-accordion__toggle" type="button" aria-expanded="false"><!--[--><div class="o-pwa-accordion__headings"><span class="o-pwa-accordion__heading"><!----> Returning Furniture &amp; Select Decor</span><span class="o-pwa-accordion__subheading"></span></div><span class="c-pwa-icon o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded"><svg version="1.1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 15 15" style="enable-background:new 0 0 15 15;" xml:space="preserve">

<ul>
<li>Unlimited Furniture Delivery items in saleable condition may be returned or exchanged within 30 days of the delivery date.</li>
<li>Unlimited Furniture Delivery items must be shipped back via our delivery service and cannot be returned to a store.</li>
<li>Select upholstery is subject to a 25% restocking fee, as well as applicable taxes. The fee will be deducted from the refund once the return is processed. For details about the restocking fee for a specific item, please visit the product page.</li>
<li>For fabric care and item maintenance, <a href="/help/furniture-guide">visit our Furniture Guide</a> for answers!</li>
</ul>
<br><br><h3>After Your Delivery: Questions &amp; Concerns</h3>
We hope everything goes perfectly, but in the event that it doesn't, please contact our Furniture Specialists to report any damages or issues. For White Glove Delivery, the delivery team should contact our Furniture Specialists before leaving. For support after unpacking doorstep or oversized UPS items, <a href="/help/contact-us">contact our Furniture Specialists</a> immediately to report any damages or concerns.
<br><br>Please refer to the <a href="/help/returns-exchanges">Returns &amp; Exchanges</a> section for our complete return policy.
<br><br><h3>How to Return Furniture</h3>
If your item(s) arrived via delivery service, please <a href="/help/contact-us">contact our furniture specialists</a> to schedule a complimentary return pickup. Your method of payment will be refunded once your items are processed at our warehouse. The initial Unlimited Furniture Delivery fee is non-refundable.
<br><br>If your oversized item(s) arrived via UPS, please <a href="/help/contact-us">contact our furniture specialists</a> to get a return label. A $5.95 shipping charge will be deducted from your refund.
<br><br><h3>Exchanges</h3>
<a href="/help/contact-us">Contact our furniture specialists</a> for assistance to complete your furniture exchange. Exchange orders may be subject to an additional flat-rate shipping fee. </div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div></div><!--]--></div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div><div id="returns-and-exchanges-6-weddings" class="c-pwa-content-module c-pwa-content-module--content-spacer-none"><div class="u-pwa-row--nested u-pwa-row" data-qa-module-length="1"><!----><!--[--><!--[--><div class="u-pwa-extra-small--12 c-pwa-content-module__component--stacked c-pwa-content-module__component"><div style="min-height:auto;" class="c-pwa-content-module__lazy-load"><!--[--><div id="5NQIeEoEdDm9iyWHXiBPMz" class="s-pwa-contentful-accordion o-pwa-contentful-accordion" isnestedgrid="true" inline="true" aligntitletogrid="false"><div class="o-pwa-accordion"><!----><h3 class="o-pwa-accordion__heading-outer"><button class="o-pwa-button-none o-pwa-accordion__toggle" type="button" aria-expanded="false"><!--[--><div class="o-pwa-accordion__headings"><span class="o-pwa-accordion__heading"><!----> Returning Wedding Gowns and Bridal Sets</span><span class="o-pwa-accordion__subheading"></span></div><span class="c-pwa-icon o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded"><svg version="1.1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 15 15" style="enable-background:new 0 0 15 15;" xml:space="preserve">

<li>We'll gladly accept unworn, unwashed, and unaltered eligible merchandise for return or exchange. Merchandise must be free of stains, pet hair, floor debris, and pin marks. </li>
<li>All original tags must be attached and items must be returned in original packaging. </li>
<li>They may not be returned to stores <em>unless originally purchased</em> at a store location. </li>
<li>Made-to-order gowns are final sale and cannot be cancelled, returned or exchanged.</li>
<li>Wedding gowns are subject to a $25 restocking fee, as well as applicable taxes. The fee will be deducted from the refund once the return is processed. For details about the restocking fee for a specific item, please visit the product page.</li>

<br><br>To start your return or exchange, either sign into your Anthropologie account, select <a href="/orders">Order History</a>, then locate and select the order you wish to return or exchange. Guest orders and orders received as gifts may be found by checking your <a href="#order-inquiry">Order Status</a>.</div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div></div><!--]--></div></div><!--]--><!--]--></div></div><div id="returns-and-exchanges-7-gifts-or-registry" class="c-pwa-content-module c-pwa-content-module--content-spacer-none"><div class="u-pwa-row--nested u-pwa-row" data-qa-module-length="1"><!----><!--[--><!--[--><div class="u-pwa-extra-small--12 c-pwa-content-module__component--stacked c-pwa-content-module__component"><div style="min-height:auto;" class="c-pwa-content-module__lazy-load"><!--[--><div id="6E8hAlsiOyOCpR3daSHYPi" class="s-pwa-contentful-accordion o-pwa-contentful-accordion" isnestedgrid="true" inline="true" aligntitletogrid="false"><div class="o-pwa-accordion"><!----><h3 class="o-pwa-accordion__heading-outer"><button class="o-pwa-button-none o-pwa-accordion__toggle" type="button" aria-expanded="false"><!--[--><div class="o-pwa-accordion__headings"><span class="o-pwa-accordion__heading"><!----> Returning Gifts or Registry Purchases</span><span class="o-pwa-accordion__subheading"></span></div><span class="c-pwa-icon o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded o-pwa-accordion__control o-pwa-accordion__control--expanded"><svg version="1.1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 15 15" style="enable-background:new 0 0 15 15;" xml:space="preserve">

Those rights and how you may exercise them are described below. The fact that you have elected to exercise these rights will have no adverse effect on the price and quality of our products.\\n\\n__RIGHT TO REQUEST INFORMATION ABOUT DISCLOSURE TO THIRD PARTIES FOR THEIR DIRECT MARKETING PURPOSES.__ You may request information about our disclosure of personal information to third parties or affiliated companies for their direct marketing purposes. To make such request, please email us at the address listed above. Please allow up to 30 days for us to process your request. You may submit such a request once per year.\\n\\n__RIGHT TO KNOW.__ You may request that we provide you for the last 12 months a list of the categories of personal information we have collected about you, the categories of sources from which it was collected, the business purpose for collecting or “selling” the information, and the categories of “third parties” to whom we disclosed or “sold” that information. You may also request that we provide you in machine readable format a copy of the specific pieces of personal information we have collected about you in the past 12 months. You may make a request to know up to two times in a 12-month period, subject to limitations described in the law. For a list of general categories of information that we have collected and shared in the past 12 months, see the tables above in the “What We Collect and Why” and “How We Share Your Data” sections.\\n\\n__RIGHT TO DELETE.__ You may request that we delete any personal information that we have collected from you. However, the law exempts certain information from deletion. For example, we may keep information necessary for security and fraud detection. We also may keep information needed to provide you goods or services. For example, if you ask us to delete your data but retain your loyalty account, we will keep the information we need to continue providing you loyalty benefits. When we respond to your request to delete, we will explain what (if any) information we have kept and why. Please note that the law does not consider anonymized or pseudonymized information to be “personal information,” and we may “delete” your information by anonymizing or pseudonymizing it.\\n\\n__NON-DISCRIMINATION.__ You have the right to be free from discrimination for using these rights. We will not deny you goods or services, charge you different rates, or give you different discounts because you used one of these rights.\\n\\n__HOW TO MAKE A REQUEST.__ You may make a request to know or delete by emailing DataPrivacyOfficer@urbn.com or you may [click here](https://www.urbn.com/privacy-policy) for our privacy compliance toll-free number. When you make a request, we will take steps to verify your identity before responding. This is to protect your information. We will ask you to provide us your email and physical address. If you maintain an account with us, these must match the addresses connected to your account. We will then send you a physical letter to this address with a one-time code. You must email us this code from the email address you provided. Once you do, we will respond to your request.\\n\\n__AUTHORIZED REPRESENTATIVES.__ You may also designate an authorized representative to make data subject rights requests on your behalf. We will require verification that you did in fact authorize the representative. Unless the law requires otherwise, your authorized representative must provide contact details for you. We will contact you to confirm that you authorized the representative. Once you confirm, we will promptly respond to the rights request.\\n\\n__CONTACT FOR MORE INFORMATION.__ If you have questions please contact DataPrivacyOfficer@urbn.com.\\n\\n__RIGHT TO OPT OUT OF “SALE” OF PERSONAL INFORMATION__\\nIf you are a California resident, you have the right to opt out of the “sale” of your personal information to “third parties.”\\n\\n*Sale is defined very broadly.* The law defines “sale” more broadly than you might think. It doesn’t just include the exchange of data for money. Instead, it covers any transfer of personal information to a “third party” in exchange for “other valuable consideration.”\\n\\nWe do not transfer your information to third parties in exchange for money and we will not do so. However, we do transfer personal information to certain third parties in order to operate our business (for example, to market our products and services). It is possible that someone could claim that this transfer was in exchange for “other valuable consideration.” We want to be careful with and respectful of your information. Therefore, if you opt out, we will not transfer your information to any “third party” except as explained below (and allowed by law).\\n\\n*Opting out may have impacts you don’t expect.* Any transfer of data to a “third party” may be considered a “sale” of data. The law considers any other business a “third party” unless that business agrees to specific contractual provisions. We try to get all of the businesses to whom we send information to add this special language to their contracts with us. But not everyone will or can agree to those terms.\\n\\nAs a result, if you opt out of the “sale” of data, we will not be able to send data to some of our business partners. That may mean that, after you opt out, you may not receive all of the marketing and other information you are accustomed to receiving from us. In the extreme, if one of our critical partners cannot agree to the special contract language, and is considered a “third party”, it is possible that some features of our web site or other services may not work for you after you opt out.\\n\\nIn addition, California law prevents us from asking you to “opt in” for a year after you “opt out.” We are allowed to tell you, however, if a specific transaction requires a transfer of data to a third party. We may alert you if a transaction requires a transfer to a third party so that you can opt in if you would like to continue with the transaction.\\n\\n*Minors.* We do not “sell” the data of people under 16, and do not intend to collect data related to people who are under 13.\\n\\n*Exceptions to the opt out.* The law creates several exceptions that are important for you to be aware of.\\n\\nWe are allowed to transfer data to a “service provider” even if you opt out. The law does not consider that kind of transfer to be a “sale.” A service provider is a business that agrees not to use your information for any purpose other than providing the services specified in our contract. For example, we transfer data to business partners to provide data security and detect fraud. Those businesses are “service providers.” Even if you opt out, we will continue to transfer data to our service providers.\\n\\nWe are also allowed to transfer your data to a third party where you direct the transfer or direct us to interact with the third party. For example, if you direct us to use a specific payment method to pay for your order, even if you have opted out, we will still transfer the data necessary to process your order.\\n\\nCollecting information and using it ourselves is not a “sale.” Opting out of the “sale” of your data does not prevent us from continuing to collect and process your personal information. You can learn more about how and why we collect personal information at our [Notice of Collection](https://www.anthropologie.com/help/privacy-policy/#ccpacollectionnotice \\\"Notice of Collection\\\").\\n\\nThe “opt out” only applies to “personal information.” This is information that is or is capable of being linked to you. We use anonymization and pseudonymization to protect privacy. That data is not “personal information.” Opting out will not limit our transfer of that data to third parties.\\n\\n*Technical limitations on opt out.* Please note that there are technical limits to our ability to identify data related to you and, if you opt out, to prevent the “sale” of that information to other parties. Where we can reasonably determine that information relates to you and you have opted out, we will not “sell” it to “third parties” (except as allowed by the law). However, we may not be able to determine that information relates to you. In that case, the information may be “sold” to a third party.\\n\\n*Opting out is not unsubscribe.* Opting out of the “sale” of personal information will not prevent you from receiving marketing messages from us. If you wish to unsubscribe from our emails, please follow the unsubscribe link in one of our emails. If you wish to unsubscribe from text messages, please follow the unsubscribe process for the program you opted into.\\n\\n__HOW TO OPT OUT__. If you would like to opt out of the “sale” of your data, you have several options. You may click the opt out button (located below), you may email us at DataPrivacyOfficer@urbn.com, or you may [click here](https://www.urbn.com/privacy-policy) for our privacy compliance toll-free number. You will not be required to create an account in order to opt out.\\n\\n*If you have an account.* The easiest way to opt out is to “log in” to your account and then click the opt out button. If you do, the email address associated with your account will be opted out, and we will set a cookie on your device to facilitate your opt out. That cookie will enable us to identify you if you return and allow us to prevent data from being “sold”. If you delete the cookie or return using a different device or browser and are not “logged in” to your account, we will not be able to connect the session to your opt out and will not be able to prevent the “sale” of the data.\\n\\nIf you want to opt out using email or our toll-free number, you will need to follow more steps. If you want to opt out an email address that is different from the email on your account, you will need to use the email process or toll-free number to do so. To use the email process or toll-free numbers to opt out, you or your authorized representative will need to provide us the email address you wish to opt out. If you want multiple email addresses opted out, you must provide each address to us. This is because we have no reasonable way to use a single address you give us to link to other addresses you may have.\\n\\nIf you email us or use the toll free number, we will have no way of setting a cookie on your device. This will limit our ability to prevent the “sale” of data. For this reason, the opt out button is the most complete way to opt out of the “sale” of data.\\n\\n*If you do not have an account.* The most complete way to opt out is to use the “opt out” button and then follow the prompts to email us the email addresses you would like opted out. If you do, we will set a cookie on your device to facilitate your opt out. That cookie will enable us to identify you if you return and allow us to prevent data from being “sold”. If you delete the cookie or return using a different device or browser, we will not be able to connect the session to your opt out and will not be able to prevent the “sale” of the data.\\n\\nAfter you click the “opt out” button, if you want to opt an email address out, you must follow the prompts and provide us the address. In the alternative, you may email us or use the toll free number. If you want multiple email addresses opted out, you must provide each address to us. This is because we have no reasonable way to use a single address you give us to link to other addresses you may have.\\n\\nIf you email us or use the toll free number, we will have no way of setting a cookie on your device.
